<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function(){
        Vue.component('cart-mini-item-adaptive', {
            props: ['item'],
            data: function(){
                return {
                    show: true
                }
            }
        });

        new Vue({
            el: '#cart-mini-top-adaptive',
            store: store,
            mixins: [VueFilters],
            mounted: function(){
                if(CartMain.$el.length) {
                    this.active = false;
                }

            computed: {
                href: function(){
                    return store.state.cart.totals.qty ? '/cart' : 'javascript://';

<div class="header-elements-cart" id="cart-mini-sticky"><a class="cart-mini-a" href="javascript://"><div class="header-element-cart-iconic"><span class="header-elements-cart-qty">0</span></div> <span class="header-elements-cart-text" style="display: none;"><span class="header-elements-cart-small-text"></span> <span class="header-elements-cart-large-text"></span></span></a> <div class="mini-cart _sticky"><div class="mini-cart__container"><div class="_scroll ps ps--theme_default" data-ps-id="bf027da6-7c8a-4865-322f-ccd5c2e5cccd"><ul></ul><div class="ps__scrollbar-x-rail" style="left: 0px; bottom: 0px;"><div class="ps__scrollbar-x" style="left: 0px; width: 0px;" tabindex="0"></div></div><div class="ps__scrollbar-y-rail" style="top: 0px; right: 0px;"><div class="ps__scrollbar-y" style="top: 0px; height: 0px;" tabindex="0"></div></div></div> <div class="_summary"><span class="_text">Всего к оплате:</span> <span class="_price">0.00</span> <a class="button" href="/cart">Перейти в корзину</a></div></div></div></div>
<script id="template-cart-mini-item" type="text/x-template">
    <li v-if="show">
        <a :href="item.UF_URL" class="_image"><img :src="item.UF_DETAIL_PICTURE" /></a>
        <a :href="item.UF_URL" class="_name">{{item.UF_NAME}}</a>
        <div class="_price">{{price}}</div>
        <div class="_quantity">{{item.UF_QTY}} шт.</div>
        <div class="_close"><button @click="deleteItem" class="_remove" :data-id="item.ID" :data-name="item.UF_NAME" :data-quantity="item.UF_QTY" :data-price="price">Удалить</button></div>
    </li>
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function(){
        Vue.component('cart-mini-item', {
            props: ['item'],
            template: '#template-cart-mini-item',
            data: function(){
                return {
                    show: true
                }
            },
            computed: {
                price: function(){
                    return parseInt(this.item.UF_GIFT_ACTION_ID) > 0 ? 'Подарок' : formatPrice(this.item.UF_PRICE_OLD, ' ', 2);
                }
            },
            methods: {
                deleteItem: function(){
                    this.show = false;
                    Cart.deleteItem(this.item.ID);
                }
            }
        });

        new Vue({
            el: '#cart-mini-sticky',
            store: store,
            mixins: [VueFilters],
            mounted: function(){
                this.initScroll();
                if(CartMain.$el.length) {
                    this.active = false;
                }
            },
            updated: function(){
                this.initScroll();
            },
            computed: {
                showList: function(){
                    return !window.isCart;
                },
                href: function(){
                    return store.state.cart.totals.qty ? '/cart' : 'javascript://';
                },
                isEmpty: function(){
                    return store.state.cart.totals.qty === 0;
                },
                totalAll: function(){
                    return store.state.cart.totals.total_all
                        ? formatPrice(store.state.cart.totals.total_all, ' ', 2, true)
                        : '';
                },
                itemsAll: function(){
                    var items = {};
                    _.each(store.state.cart.unavailable, function(item, id){ items[id] = item; });
                    _.each(store.state.cart.items, function(item, id){ items[id] = item; });
                    return items;
                }
            },
            methods: {
                initScroll: function(){
                    var $el = $(this.$el).find('.mini-cart ._scroll');
                    $el.perfectScrollbar({
                        minScrollbarLength: 50,
                        maxScrollbarLength: 50
                    });
                }
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        Menu.init();

        $('.head-menu-items').on('mouseenter', function() {
          var pictArr = this.getElementsByClassName('lazyPapa');

          if (pictArr[0].hasAttribute("data-src")) {
            for (var i = 0; i < pictArr.length; i++) {
              pictArr[i].setAttribute('src', pictArr[i].getAttribute('data-src'));
              pictArr[i].onload = function(EO) {
                EO.target.attributes.removeNamedItem("data-src");
              };
            }
          }

        });

    });

        $(document).ready(function () {
            (new Banner()).init('5db342fdd0641', {
                nav: true,
                dots: true            });


          $('.ert .owl-item.active .lazyBanner').each(function() {
            this.setAttribute('src', this.getAttribute('data-src'));
            this.onload = function(EO) {
              EO.target.attributes.removeNamedItem("data-src");
            };
          });


          $('.ert .owl-item').each(function() {
            var self = this;
            $(self).bind('active', function() {
              var inImg = $(self).find('.lazyBanner'); //массив элементов, по сути дина 1
              if (inImg[0].hasAttribute("data-src")) {
                inImg[0].setAttribute('src', inImg[0].getAttribute('data-src'));
                inImg[0].onload = function(EO) {
                  EO.target.attributes.removeNamedItem("data-src");
                };
              }
            });
          });

        });
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function () {
            (new Banner()).init('5db342feea99d', {
                nav: true,
                dots: true            });


          $('.ert .owl-item.active .lazyBanner').each(function() {
            this.setAttribute('src', this.getAttribute('data-src'));
            this.onload = function(EO) {
              EO.target.attributes.removeNamedItem("data-src");
            };
          });


          $('.ert .owl-item').each(function() {
            var self = this;
            $(self).bind('active', function() {
              var inImg = $(self).find('.lazyBanner'); //массив элементов, по сути дина 1
              if (inImg[0].hasAttribute("data-src")) {
                inImg[0].setAttribute('src', inImg[0].getAttribute('data-src'));
                inImg[0].onload = function(EO) {
                  EO.target.attributes.removeNamedItem("data-src");
                };
              }
            });
          });

        });
    </script>

            });
